LAUNCHES: 30 of 30, 10000000 messages, 3 epochs, 10 arms
RECORDS : 900, all carrying msg_cycles, msg_instructions, msg_l1d_miss,

ELAPSED : 3m 27s
PRIVILEGE: run under sudo, which the kperf counters require

WHY THIS FILE EXISTS. H76 replicated the barrier cross with every raw
record retained, but without the PMU, because the counters need root and
an unattended script must not carry a password prompt. It therefore
compared nanosecond ratios, which is fair only if the arms run at similar
frequency. The original run asserted 4.035 to 4.414 GHz; H76 could not
check it, so that assumption stood unverified underneath the paper's first
three contributions. This file carries core_ghz per record, which settles
it. See docs/defects.md D4. Verdicts are not recorded here: the analysis
runs after the data is committed.
